# Commands · R · part 1

1,454 entries, r-audit to resources. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **r-audit**: You are running the /r-audit full codebase audit. This command dispatches multiple reviewer and qa subagents in parallel to scan the entire codebase for latent issues, then synthesizes findings into a structured AUDIT.md. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-audit.md) · ★19)
- **r-audit-slop**: AI slop audit. Finds dead code, orphan helpers, noop functions, tautological guards, redundant implementations, and over-engineered abstractions. Read-only static analysis. Uses a discovery-first protocol to build a per-project call-site manifest before scanning — prevents the false-positive wall that hits naive dead-code scanners when projects use runtime-sourced templates, CLI dispatch tables, o ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-audit-slop.md) · ★19)
- **r-build**: You are the build command. You prepare context and dispatch the dispatcher subagent to execute an implementation plan. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-build.md) · ★19)
- **r-caller**: You are running the /r-caller command. This is a test fixture. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/canonical/commands/r-caller.md) · ★19)
- **r-context-audit**: Measure, score, and report on Claude Code context overhead for this workspace. Identifies bloat, tracks drift from baseline, and produces actionable optimization recommendations. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-context-audit.md) · ★19)
- **r-example**: You are running the /r-example command. This is a test fixture. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/canonical/commands/r-example.md) · ★19)

- **r-init**: Governance initialization for any codebase. Scans the project, ingests existing convention files, detects languages and tooling, and generates supplementary governance under `.rdf/governance/`. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-init.md) · ★19)
- **r-lsp-diagnose**: Diagnose R language server issues and suggest fixes ([jsperger/llm-r-skills](https://github.com/jsperger/llm-r-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/r-lsp-diagnose.md) · ★15)
- **r-mode**: Switch the operational mode for the current session. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-mode.md) · ★19)
- **r-plan**: Take a spec (or equivalent input) and decompose it into an execution-grade implementation plan written to `docs/plans/{YYYY-MM-DD}-{topic}-plan.md`. You never write code. You produce plans. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-plan.md) · ★19)
- **r-refresh**: You are running the /r-refresh governance refresh. This command re-scans the codebase and updates governance files to match current reality, preserving user modifications. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-refresh.md) · ★19)
- **r-review**: You are the review command. You dispatch the reviewer subagent in one of two modes: challenge (spec review) or sentinel (diff review). ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-review.md) · ★19)
- **r-review-answer**: Route every finding in a sentinel or challenge report to a structured response — FIX, REBUT, or DEFER — and write an audit-trail artifact. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-review-answer.md) · ★19)
- **r-save**: End-of-session state sync. Updates tracking files to reflect what actually happened, persists a session summary for the next agent. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-save.md) · ★19)
- **r-ship**: You are running the /r-ship release workflow. This is a composite workflow that verifies readiness, dispatches quality gates, prepares release artifacts, and publishes a PR. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-ship.md) · ★19)
- **r-spec**: Guide the user from idea to architecture-grade spec through research-driven collaborative dialogue. You never write code. You produce design documents. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-spec.md) · ★19)
- **r-start**: Reload context and display project health. This is the recommended first command in any session. It gives the agent a warm handoff — enough context to continue where the last session left off. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-start.md) · ★19)
- **r-status**: Display a structured overview of project health: plan progress, phase outcomes, governance freshness, and recent activity. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-status.md) · ★19)
- **r-sync**: You are running the /r-sync canonical source synchronization. This is an RDF-internal command that pulls emergency edits made directly to ~/.claude/ back into the rdf canonical source tree. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-sync.md) · ★19)
- **r-tasks**: Display the current task list state. Use this when a long-running command seems stuck, or to check progress at any time. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-tasks.md) · ★19)
- **r-test**: You are the test command. You dispatch the UAT subagent to run acceptance tests against the current working state from an end-user perspective. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-test.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-chg-dedup**: Deduplicate changelog entries for the current version. Scans CHANGELOG and CHANGELOG.RELEASE for duplicates, overlapping entries, tracking artifacts, fluff, and section proliferation. Report mode by default — only modify files when explicitly told to fix. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-chg-dedup.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-chg-gen**: Generate changelog entries from the current diff or commit range. Read governance for changelog format rules. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-chg-gen.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-ci-gen**: Generate a GitHub Actions CI workflow from the project's test infrastructure. Creates workflow files for user review — does NOT commit. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-ci-gen.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-claudemd-review**: Analyze recent conversations to find improvements for CLAUDE.md files, and audit existing CLAUDE.md content for prose drift and memory-bound state contamination. Read-only report — no modifications. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-claudemd-review.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-code-map**: Structural map of a source file — classes, functions, methods with line ranges. Reduces context cost when an agent needs one symbol from a large file by letting it read only the relevant line range. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-code-map.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-code-modernize**: Assess codebase modernization maturity and generate a phased remediation plan. This is a **read-only assessment** — it does NOT modify any project files. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-code-modernize.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-code-scan**: Structured pattern-class bug finder. Searches project files for instances of known anti-patterns or a custom pattern. Groups results by file and function, cross-references AUDIT.md for tracked instances. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-code-scan.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-doc-gen**: Generate publication-ready documentation for a specific surface of the current project. Reads existing content, applies project documentation standards from governance, ensures cross-surface consistency, and presents results for user review before applying changes. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-doc-gen.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-lib-release**: Shared library release lifecycle. Handles pre-flight checks, release preparation, and consumer drift notification. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-lib-release.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-lib-sync**: Cross-project shared library drift detection. Compares canonical library sources against all consumer copies. Report mode by default; optional sync mode copies and verifies. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-lib-sync.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-mem-audit**: Stale memory and contradiction detection. Reads MEMORY.md files and cross-references every factual claim against live project state. Read-only report — no modifications. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-mem-audit.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-mem-compact**: Archive stale MEMORY.md entries. Detects staleness, identifies completed work ready for archival, and proposes compaction. Preview mode by default — only modifies files when user explicitly confirms. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-mem-compact.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-proj-cross**: Cross-project analysis for workspace project sets. Reads MEMORY.md, PLAN.md, AUDIT.md, and CLAUDE.md from all projects to identify shared patterns, duplicate effort, convention drift, and alignment opportunities. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-proj-cross.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-rel-squash**: Generate a squash plan for the current release branch by grouping commits into logical buckets aligned with CHANGELOG.RELEASE entries. Default: plan summary only. Pass `--apply` to execute after review. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-rel-squash.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-test-dedup**: Find duplicate, overlapping, tautological, dead, and low-value tests in the project's test suite. Read-only static analysis — does not modify any files or execute the test suite. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-test-dedup.md) · ★19)
- **r-util-test-scope**: Analyze the current diff to recommend which tests to run and map changed functions to their test files. Combines test tier selection with function-to-test impact analysis. Read-only — does not execute tests. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-util-test-scope.md) · ★19)
- **r-verify**: You are the verify command. You dispatch the QA subagent to run verification checks against the current working state or a specified scope. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-verify.md) · ★19)
- **r-verify-claim**: Verify a falsifiable claim about the current state of the codebase, a commit, or a file. Produces a structured triage report with probe commands, results, and a PASS/FAIL/UNVERIFIABLE verdict. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-verify-claim.md) · ★19)
- **r-vpe**: Optional end-to-end pipeline orchestrator. Takes outcome-oriented conversation from the user and manages the full spec → plan → build → ship workflow. ([rfxn/rdf](https://github.com/rfxn/rdf/blob/HEAD/canonical/commands/r-vpe.md) · ★19)
